Original source text:
Where equipment required by SOLAS chapter V or this chapter has sensors that project below the hull, such sensors shall be protected against ice.
Normalized requirement:
IF required_sensor_projects_below_hull = true, THEN sensor_ice_protection_present = true.
Required inputs: Required-sensor inventory, projection location, and ice protection

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO_084_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredSensorProjectsBelowHull ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The below-hull sensor projection status must be recorded as exactly one boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredSensorProjectsBelowHull ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:sensorIceProtectionPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Sensors projecting below the hull shall be protected against ice."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredSensorProjectsBelowHull ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO084Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredSensorProjectsBelowHull ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must state whether required sensors project below the hull."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When required sensors project below the hull, sensor ice protection must be present." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:requiredSensorProjectsBelowHull true .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:sensorIceProtectionPresent true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

REQUIREMENT 022: IMO-038

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 22
Batch: BATCH_03
Requirement ID: IMO-038
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 4.3.2.2 / Part I-A Chapter 4 - Damaged stability / 19
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-038-20260915T104334135378Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The ice damage extents to be assumed when demonstrating compliance with paragraph 4.3.2.1 shall be such that: the longitudinal extent is 4.5% of the upper ice waterline length if centred forward of the maximum breadth on the upper ice waterline, and 1.5% of upper ice waterline length otherwise, and shall be assumed at any longitudinal position along the ship's length; the transverse penetration extent is 760 mm, measured normal to the shell over the full extent of the damage; and the vertical extent is the lesser of 20% of the upper ice waterline draught or the longitudinal extent, and shall be assumed at any vertical position between the keel and 120% of the upper ice waterline draught.
Normalized requirement:
longitudinal_damage_extent = 0.045 * upper_ice_waterline_length when damage_center_forward_of_max_breadth = true, otherwise 0.015 * upper_ice_waterline_length; transverse_penetration_mm = 760; vertical_damage_extent = min(0.20 * upper_ice_waterline_draught, longitudinal_damage_extent); allowed_vertical_position is between keel and 1.20 * upper_ice_waterline_draught.
Required inputs: Damage scenario geometry and calculated extents

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:damageCenterForwardOfMaxBreadth ?forward ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineLength ?lengthNode ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ?draughtNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalDamageExtent ?longNode ;
                      nltl:verticalDamageExtent ?verticalNode ;
                      nltl:selectedVerticalDamagePosition ?positionNode .

                ?lengthNode qudt:numericValue ?length ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught ;
                             qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                          qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                ?verticalNode qudt:numericValue ?vertical ;
                              qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                ?positionNode qudt:numericValue ?position ;
                               qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                FILTER (
                    ?longitudinal != IF(
                        ?forward,
                        0.045 * ?length,
                        0.015 * ?length
                    )
                    ||
                    ?vertical != IF(
                        0.20 * ?draught < ?longitudinal,
                        0.20 * ?draught,
                        ?longitudinal
                    )
                    ||
                    ?position < 0
                    ||
                    ?position > 1.20 * ?draught
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:path nltl:damageCenterForwardOfMaxBreadth ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Damage-center position indicator is required." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineLength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalDamageExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:transversePenetration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "760"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "760"^^xsd:decimal ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalDamageExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedVerticalDamagePosition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Longitudinal and vertical damage extents must satisfy the specified piecewise formulas." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:damageCenterForwardOfMaxBreadth ?forward ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineLength ?lengthNode ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ?draughtNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalDamageExtent ?longNode ;
                      nltl:verticalDamageExtent ?verticalNode .

                ?lengthNode qudt:numericValue ?length .
                ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught .
                ?longNode qudt:numericValue ?longitudinal .
                ?verticalNode qudt:numericValue ?vertical .

                BIND(IF(?forward = true,
                        "0.045"^^xsd:decimal * ?length,
                        "0.015"^^xsd:decimal * ?length) AS ?expectedLongitudinal)
                BIND(IF("0.20"^^xsd:decimal * ?draught < ?expectedLongitudinal,
                        "0.20"^^xsd:decimal * ?draught,
                        ?expectedLongitudinal) AS ?expectedVertical)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?longitudinal - ?expectedLongitudinal) >
                    IF(
                        ABS(?expectedLongitudinal) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal >
                        "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        ABS(?expectedLongitudinal) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                    ||
                    ABS(?vertical - ?expectedVertical) >
                    IF(
                        ABS(?expectedVertical) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal >
                        "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        ABS(?expectedVertical) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal,
                        "0.000000001"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Selected vertical damage position must be between the keel and 120% of upper ice waterline draught." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedVerticalDamagePosition ?positionNode ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ?draughtNode .

                ?positionNode qudt:numericValue ?position .
                ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught .

                FILTER (
                    ?position < "0"^^xsd:decimal
                    ||
                    ?position > ("1.20"^^xsd:decimal * ?draught)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                    ?longitudinal > (?expectedLongitudinal + ?longitudinalTolerance) ||
                    ?vertical < (?expectedVertical - ?verticalTolerance) ||
                    ?vertical > (?expectedVertical + ?verticalTolerance) ||
                    ?position < 0.0 ||

                )
```

Original source text:
Discharge into the sea of food wastes is only permitted when the ship is as far as practicable from areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10, but in any case not less than 12 nautical miles from the nearest land, nearest ice-shelf, or nearest fast ice.
Normalized requirement:
IF operating_area = Arctic_waters AND food_waste_discharge_to_sea = true, THEN distance_to_nearest_land_ice_shelf_or_fast_ice_nm >= 12 and the distance from areas with ice_concentration_tenths > 1 shall be recorded.
Required inputs: Discharge record, position, nearest-feature distances, and ice data

Locked context, definitions, ownership, units, and dependency contract:
{
  "dependency_contract": {
    "applicabilityTerms": [
      "operatingArea",
      "arcticWaters",
      "foodWasteDischargeToSea"
    ],
    "auditFlags": [],
    "comparisonModel": "conditional inclusive distance threshold and distinct recorded concentration-area distance",
    "comparisonTerms": [],
    "controlledValueTerms": [],

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-120> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When operating area is Arctic_waters and food waste is discharged to sea, the nearest land, ice-shelf, or fast-ice distance must be at least 12 nautical miles, and a recorded distance from an area with ice concentration above one tenth must be provided." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:operatingArea ?area ;
                      nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ?discharge .
                FILTER (
                    STR(?area) = "Arctic_waters" &&
                    ?discharge = true
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:distanceToNearestLandIceShelfOrFastIce ?nearest ;
                          nltl:nearbyIceConcentration ?nearby ;
                          nltl:distanceFromAreaWithIceConcentrationAboveOneTenth ?recorded .

                    ?nearest a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                             qudt:numericValue ?nearestValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:MI_N .
                    ?nearby a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?nearbyValue ;
                            qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                    ?recorded a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                             qudt:numericValue ?recordedValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:MI_N .

                    FILTER (
                        isNumeric(?nearestValue) &&
                        isNumeric(?nearbyValue) &&
                        isNumeric(?recordedValue) &&
                        ?nearestValue >= 12 &&
                        ?nearbyValue > 1
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-120> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:hasValue "Arctic_waters"^^xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship's operating area must be recorded as Arctic_waters."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:arcticWaters ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:hasValue "Arctic_waters"^^xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Food waste discharge to sea must be recorded as true for this applicable rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When operating in Arctic_waters and discharging food waste to sea, the distance to the nearest land ice shelf or fast ice must be at least 12 nautical miles, a nearby ice concentration exceeding one tenth must be recorded, and the distance from that area must be recorded." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:operatingArea ?area ;
        nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ?discharge .
  FILTER (
    ?area = "Arctic_waters"^^xsd:string &&
    ?discharge = true
  )
  FILTER (
    NOT EXISTS {
      $this nltl:distanceToNearestLandIceShelfOrFastIce ?nearestDistance .
      ?nearestDistance a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                       qudt:numericValue ?nearestValue ;
                       qudt:unit unit:MI_N .
      FILTER (
        datatype(?nearestValue) = xsd:decimal &&
        ?nearestValue >= 12
      )
    } ||
    NOT EXISTS {
      $this nltl:nearbyIceConcentration ?nearbyConcentration .
      ?nearbyConcentration a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?nearbyValue ;
                            qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
      FILTER (
        datatype(?nearbyValue) = xsd:decimal &&
        ?nearbyValue > 1
      )
    } ||
    NOT EXISTS {
      $this nltl:distanceFromAreaWithIceConcentrationAboveOneTenth ?areaDistance .
      ?areaDistance a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    qudt:numericValue ?areaDistanceValue ;
                    qudt:unit unit:MI_N .
      FILTER ( datatype(?areaDistanceValue) = xsd:decimal )
    }
  )
}
"""
    ] .
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-060-20260915T132341413727Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The section modulus of a web frame shall be calculated using Z = (M/sigma_y)*sqrt(1/(1 - (gamma*A/A_a)^2))*10^6 [cm^3] (4.17), where M is the maximum calculated bending moment under ice load F and shall be M = 0.193*F*L, gamma is selected from Table 4-8, A is the required shear area, and A_a = A_f + A_w is the actual cross-sectional area of the web frame.
Normalized requirement:
Compute M_MNm = 0.193*F_MN*L_m and A_a_cm2 = A_f_cm2 + A_w_cm2. Then compute Z_cm3 = (M_MNm/sigma_y_N_mm2)*sqrt(1/(1 - (gamma*A_required_cm2/A_a_cm2)^2))*10^6, with gamma selected from Table 4-8.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA

Original source text:
Ships operating in areas and during periods where ice accretion is likely to occur shall be: designed to minimize the accretion of ice; and equipped with such means for removing ice as the Administration may require; for example, electrical and pneumatic devices, and/or special tools such as axes or wooden clubs for removing ice from bulwarks, rails and erections.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_accretion_likely = true, THEN ice_accretion_minimization_design_present = true AND required_ice_removal_means_present = true.
Required inputs: Operating-area assessment, design evidence, and required ice-removal equipment inventory

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionMinimizationDesignPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "When ice accretion is likely, the ship must have a design that minimizes ice accretion."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredIceRemovalMeansPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "When ice accretion is likely, the ship must have the required means for removing ice."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:administrationRequiredIceRemovalMeans ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "When ice accretion is likely, the ship must identify the ice-removal means required by the Administration."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

REQUIREMENT 026: TRF-043
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 26
Batch: BATCH_03
Requirement ID: TRF-043
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 4.4.1 / Vertical extension of ice strengthening for framing / 18
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-043-20260915T125749923794Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The vertical extension of the ice strengthening of framing shall be at least as given in Table 4- 6. Where an upper bow ice belt is required (see 4.3.1), the ice-strengthened part of the framing shall be extended to at least the top of this ice belt. Where the ice-strengthening would go beyond a deck, the top or bottom plating of a tank or tank top by no more than 250 mm, it can be terminated at that deck, top or bottom plating of the tank or tank top. Table 4-6 values: IA Super framing above UIWL 1.2 m; below LIWL bow down to tank top or below top of floors, midbody 2.0 m, stern 1.6 m. IA, IB and IC framing above UIWL 1.0 m; below LIWL bow 1.6 m, midbody 1.3 m, stern 1.0 m.
Normalized requirement:
Set the vertical extent of framing ice strengthening from Table 4-6. IF upper_bow_ice_belt_required = true, THEN framing_ice_strengthening_upper_extent >= upper_bow_ice_belt_top. IF extension_beyond_adjacent_deck_or_tank_boundary_mm <= 250, THEN termination_at_that_boundary is permitted. Table 4-6 values: IA Super framing above UIWL 1.2 m; below LIWL bow down to tank top or below top of floors, midbody 2.0 m, stern 1.6 m. IA, IB and IC framing above UIWL 1.0 m; below LIWL bow 1.6 m, midbody 1.3 m, stern 1.0 m.

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasFramingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
        sh:class nltl:framingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-TerminationCaseShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasComponent ;
        sh:class nltl:frame ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-FrameShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-TerminationCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:framingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:extensionBeyondAdjacentDeckOrTankBoundary ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:terminationAdjacentBoundary ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:class nltl:deckStructure ]
            [ sh:class nltl:tankBoundaryPlating ]
            [ sh:class nltl:tankTop ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningTerminationAtAdjacentBoundaryPermitted ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the extension is no more than 250 mm, termination at the adjacent boundary must be permitted." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:extensionBeyondAdjacentDeckOrTankBoundary ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?extension ;
                          qudt:unit unit:MilliM .
                FILTER (?extension <= 250)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceStrengtheningTerminationAtAdjacentBoundaryPermitted true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-FrameShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIa ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIb ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIc ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullRegion ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:bowRegion ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:midbodyRegion ]
            [ sh:hasValue nltl:sternRegion ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningAboveUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningBelowLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:reachesTankTopOrBelowFloorTop ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:reachesUpperBowIceBeltTop ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperBowIceBeltRequired ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The framing extent above the upper ice waterline must meet the Table 4-6 minimum for the recorded ice class." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:framingIceStrengtheningAboveUpperIceWaterline ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?extent ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper && ?extent < 1.2) ||
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIa && ?extent < 1.0) ||
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIb && ?extent < 1.0) ||
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIc && ?extent < 1.0)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The framing extent below the lower ice waterline must meet the Table 4-6 minimum for the recorded ice class and hull region." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:hullRegion ?region ;
                      nltl:framingIceStrengtheningBelowLowerIceWaterline ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?extent ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                        ?region = nltl:bowRegion &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:reachesTankTopOrBelowFloorTop true .
                        }
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                        ?region = nltl:midbodyRegion &&
                        ?extent < 2.0
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                        ?region = nltl:sternRegion &&
                        ?extent < 1.6
                    ) ||
                    (
                        (
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIa ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIb ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIc
                        ) &&
                        ?region = nltl:bowRegion &&
                        ?extent < 1.6
                    ) ||
                    (
                        (
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIa ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIb ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIc
                        ) &&
                        ?region = nltl:midbodyRegion &&
                        ?extent < 1.3
                    ) ||
                    (
                        (
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIa ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIb ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIc
                        ) &&
                        ?region = nltl:sternRegion &&
                        ?extent < 1.0
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When an upper bow ice belt is required, the framing must reach its top." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:upperBowIceBeltRequired true .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:reachesUpperBowIceBeltTop true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasFramingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
        sh:class nltl:framingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-TerminationCaseShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-TerminationCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:framingIceStrengtheningTerminationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:extensionBeyondAdjacentDeckOrTankBoundary ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-ExtensionQuantityShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:terminationAdjacentBoundary ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [ sh:class nltl:deckStructure ]
            [ sh:class nltl:tankBoundaryPlating ]
            [ sh:class nltl:tankTop ]
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningTerminationAtAdjacentBoundaryPermitted ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Termination at the adjacent boundary must be permitted when the extension is no more than 250 mm." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:extensionBeyondAdjacentDeckOrTankBoundary ?extension .
                ?extension qudt:numericValue ?extensionValue .
                ?extension qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
                FILTER ( ?extensionValue <= "250"^^xsd:decimal )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceStrengtheningTerminationAtAdjacentBoundaryPermitted true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-ExtensionQuantityShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-FrameShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullRegion ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:bowRegion
            nltl:midbodyRegion
            nltl:sternRegion
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningAboveUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-MetreQuantityShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningBelowLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-MetreQuantityShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:reachesTankTopOrBelowFloorTop ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperBowIceBeltRequired ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:upperBowIceBeltRequired ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:upperBowIceBeltRequired ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:reachesUpperBowIceBeltTop ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Framing vertical extents do not satisfy the applicable Table 4-6 values." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:hullRegion ?region ;
                      nltl:framingIceStrengtheningAboveUpperIceWaterline ?above ;
                      nltl:framingIceStrengtheningBelowLowerIceWaterline ?below .

                ?above qudt:numericValue ?aboveValue ;
                       qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                ?below qudt:numericValue ?belowValue ;
                       qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                FILTER (
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper && ?aboveValue < "1.2"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass IN (nltl:iceClassIa, nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc) &&
                     ?aboveValue < "1.0"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                     ?region IN (nltl:bowRegion, nltl:midbodyRegion) &&
                     ?belowValue < "2.0"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                     ?region = nltl:sternRegion &&
                     ?belowValue < "1.6"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass IN (nltl:iceClassIa, nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc) &&
                     ?region = nltl:bowRegion &&
                     ?belowValue < "1.6"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass IN (nltl:iceClassIa, nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc) &&
                     ?region = nltl:midbodyRegion &&
                     ?belowValue < "1.3"^^xsd:decimal) ||
                    (?iceClass IN (nltl:iceClassIa, nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc) &&
                     ?region = nltl:sternRegion &&
                     ?belowValue < "1.0"^^xsd:decimal)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-043-MetreQuantityShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;

    ] .
```

Original source text:
Food wastes shall be comminuted or ground and shall be capable of passing through a screen with openings no greater than 25 mm. Food wastes shall not be contaminated by any other garbage type.
Normalized requirement:
IF food_waste_discharge_to_sea = true, THEN food_waste_comminuted_or_ground = true, required_screen_opening_mm <= 25, and contaminated_by_other_garbage = false.
Required inputs: Food-waste processing record, screen specification, and contamination status

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .

@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_121_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Food-waste discharge to sea status must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:foodWasteComminutedOrGround ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Food waste discharged to sea must be comminuted or ground."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredScreenOpening ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "25"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The screen opening for food waste discharged to sea must be no greater than 25 mm."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:contaminatedByOtherGarbage ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "Food waste discharged to sea must not be contaminated by other garbage."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:foodWasteDischargeToSea ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Fire pumps, including emergency fire pumps, water mist and water spray pumps, shall be located in compartments maintained above freezing.

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_057_StaticShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Every linked fire pump must have a containing compartment." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasComponent ?pump .
                ?pump a ?pumpType .
                FILTER (
                    ?pumpType IN (
                        nltl:firePump,
                        nltl:emergencyFirePump,
                        nltl:waterMistPump,
                        nltl:waterSprayPump
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?pump nltl:hasContainingCompartment ?compartment .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Each containing compartment of a linked fire pump must be maintained above 0 degrees Celsius." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasComponent ?pump .
                ?pump a ?pumpType .
                FILTER (
                    ?pumpType IN (
                        nltl:firePump,
                        nltl:emergencyFirePump,
                        nltl:waterMistPump,
                        nltl:waterSprayPump
                    )
                )
                ?pump nltl:hasContainingCompartment ?compartment .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?compartment a nltl:compartment ;
                        nltl:maintainedTemperature ?quantity .
                    ?quantity a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                        qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                        qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .
                    FILTER (
                        ?value > 0
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO057ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Every fire pump must have a containing compartment with maintained temperature above 0 deg C." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasComponent ?pump .
                ?pump a ?pumpType .
                FILTER (
                    ?pumpType IN (
                        nltl:firePump,
                        nltl:emergencyFirePump,
                        nltl:waterMistPump,
                        nltl:waterSprayPump
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?pump nltl:hasContainingCompartment ?compartment .
                    ?compartment nltl:maintainedTemperature ?quantity .
                    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                              qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value > 0
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_006_Static_MediumFirstYearIceThicknessShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceTypeClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:iceTypeValue
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceThickness ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Ships classified as medium first-year ice must provide an ice thickness from 0.7 m through 1.2 m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:mediumFirstYearIce .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceThickness ?thickness .
                    ?thickness a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                               qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                               qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value >= "0.7"^^xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value <= "1.2"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-006-StaticShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceTypeClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ship must provide an ice type classification to determine applicability of the medium first-year ice thickness range."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For medium first-year ice, ice thickness must be a decimal quantity in metres between 0.7 m and 1.2 m, inclusive." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:mediumFirstYearIce .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceThickness ?thickness .
                    ?thickness qudt:numericValue ?value .
                    ?thickness qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value >= "0.7"^^xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value <= "1.2"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 30
Batch: BATCH_03
Requirement ID: IMO-012
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 1.2.11 / Part I-A Chapter 1 - Definitions / 14
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-012-20260915T103006282285Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Polar Service Temperature (PST) means a temperature specified for a ship which is intended to operate in low air temperature, which shall be set at least 10 deg C below the lowest MDLT for the intended area and season of operation in polar waters.
Normalized requirement:
PST_degC <= lowest_MDLT_degC - 10.
Required inputs: Specified PST and lowest MDLT for the intended area and season

Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: PST more conservative than 10 C below MDLT.
Source clause: Part I-A 1.2.11
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/IMO/IMO-012/IMO-012-P02.ttl
RDF SHA-256: c5689e33beb690cac4c47c64cb85cea46f255e25628539e63de0b04c9cf6fc5c
Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/imo-batch-a/IMO-012-P02/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:lowestMdlt ex:lowestMdltValue ;
    nltl:polarServiceTemperature ex:polarServiceTemperatureValue .

ex:lowestMdltValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue -15.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .

ex:polarServiceTemperatureValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue -30.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .


```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix g: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

g:IMO_012_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lowestMdlt ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarServiceTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Polar service temperature must be at least 10 deg C below the lowest MDLT." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:lowestMdlt ?mdltQuantity ;
                      nltl:polarServiceTemperature ?pstQuantity .
                ?mdltQuantity qudt:numericValue ?mdltValue .
                ?pstQuantity qudt:numericValue ?pstValue .
                FILTER (?pstValue > (?mdltValue - 10))
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-012> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lowestMdlt ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarServiceTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Polar service temperature must be at least 10 degrees Celsius below the lowest MDLT." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:lowestMdlt ?mdltQuantity ;
                      nltl:polarServiceTemperature ?pstQuantity .
                ?mdltQuantity qudt:numericValue ?mdlt .
                ?pstQuantity qudt:numericValue ?pst .
                FILTER (?pst > (?mdlt - 10))
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
                      nltl:polarServiceTemperature ?pstQuantity .
```
